## D-19606 Early Titles, Travelers, and Names

Reviewed the 21 open decisions D-457 through D-483 (default IDs 2612-2695)
as grouped title, vocative, faction, place, and dialogue contexts. Four
findings were accepted and corrected (IDs 2630, 2632, 2648, 2680); the other
17 closed as `not an issue`. Previously decided D-458, D-459, D-464-D-466,
and D-478 were linked without recounting. The Korean manual's printed pages
13-14 establish `트래블러(Travelers)`. English/Japanese/Chinese were compared
directly, with German/Spanish/French/Italian checks for the relevant epithet
and landmark. No PAK evidence was used.

| ID | Previous Korean | Approved Korean |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| 2630 | `...시녀(The Maid)가 그 자리를 차지하고 있어...` | `...윈더미어의 시녀(Maid of Windemere)가 그들 대신 자리를 차지하고 있어...` |
| 2632 | `도브(Dove)` / `트래블러스(Travelers)` | `친구` / `트래블러(Travelers)` |
| 2648 | `죽어라, 롱레그(longlegs)!` | `죽어라, 다리 긴 녀석!` |
| 2680 | `이게 바로 이빨(The Teeth)...` | `여기가 바로 나로스의 이빨(Teeth of Naros)이군...` |

The ID 2632 vocative edit was already active at resumption and is recorded

## Plagueshield Context Review D-19570

The 30 open findings were resolved as 25 accepted and five `not an issue`.
The general medicine/supply is rendered `역병 방호제`. `Pledgeshield` stays
distinct; ID 63752's mistaken rank reference and ID 64448's person reading
were corrected. ID 60756 was also corrected from `플래그실드` to
`역병 방호제`, superseding its prior `not an issue` disposition. The
established tincture-item title at IDs 19347 and 25484 remains unchanged.

| ID | Previous Korean | Applied Korean | Result |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| 3761 | `플라그실드(Plagueshield)` | `역병 방호제(Plagueshield)` | accepted; context identifies a medicinal supply used to treat the village |
| 4574 | `역병 방패(Plagueshield)` | `역병 방호제(Plagueshield)` | accepted; the speaker explicitly calls for medicine in parcels |
| 7553 | `역병 방호막(Plagueshield)이 도움이 됐어.` | `역병 방호제(Plagueshield)가 도움이 됐어.` | accepted; normalize the remedy and use the particle required by the Korean term |
| 8947 | `잃어버린 역병방패를 모아라` | `잃어버린 역병 방호제를 모아라` | accepted |
| 9871, 15337, 40597 | `역병 방패` | `역병 방호제` | accepted; standardize the standalone item labels |
| 10361 | `역병 방패는 그게 전부군.` | `역병 방호제는 그게 전부군.` | accepted; the line describes the remaining supply |
| 12465 | `역병 방패(Plagueshield)` | `역병 방호제(Plagueshield)` | accepted; explicitly used to treat sick people |
| 12914 | `역병 방호(Plagueshield)` | `역병 방호제(Plagueshield)` | accepted |
| 16063, 28211 | `역병 방패(Plagueshield)` | `역병 방호제(Plagueshield)` | accepted |
| 31671 | `역병 방지제` | `역병 방호제` | accepted; align the stocked remedy with the established Plagueshield term |

| 33724 | `플레이그실드 꾸러미 세 개` | `역병 방호제 꾸러미 세 개` | accepted |

## Review Rule Correction and Source-Only Checkpoint (D-19564)

Review instructions now prohibit PAK/PAK-derived evidence, treat English as
the semantic anchor, use Japanese for voice/register and Chinese as support,
and require definite dispositions for source-faithful items. `uncertain` is
reserved for material ambiguity that remains after one focused review.
Repeated negative searches and automatic hold carry-forward are prohibited.
The D-19560 PAK rationale and D-19563 PAK-extraction search are superseded.

## Grouped `Argine, Queen of Staves` Review D-19445

The sixteen exact repeated source labels were checked together, including
their English, Japanese, Chinese, Korean, and prior decisions. The repeated
title does not resolve whether it is a tarot/card designation or character
title, nor does it establish canonical Korean name/suit/display forms.
D-10940 only removed a redundant `홀` in one separate row. Keep all sixteen
findings uncertain and make no CSV edit. The five-cell normalization proposed
in the previous handoff was not evidence-supported.

Totals remain **2,282 accepted / 24,978 `not an issue` / 11,264 uncertain**
(38,524). Active default SHA-256 remains
`7feb04d2ef991183e310657f610ce2ae67b14b0f6cbe9e57dc90327ff7511ecb`.
Report:
`consolidated-context-review-default-argine-queen-staves-20261012.md`.

## Grouped Hold Follow-ups D-19447-D-19450

These evidence-led follow-ups rechecked the ten isolated `House of Sorrows`
labels without applying one Korean form globally. Context did not settle the
canonical name, so all ten remain uncertain and unchanged. D-19448 cleared
46626 and 46650 as `not an issue`; ID 46628 had already been cleared under
D-19446. D-19449 cleared eight faithful warning/stat labels while retaining
12637 and 44240 for unresolved stat/category terminology. ID 23907 was
already cleared under D-18746 and was not recounted. D-19450 cleared the
accurate Korean rendering at 40530. ID 20481 was already cleared under
D-18288; a stale D-19442 note was corrected.

No Korean CSV cell changed in these follow-ups; no backup was needed.
Active default SHA-256 remains
`cc8c2706805482a13a2bd44a8d58687b1804a8f06bea3c6632b47639f20073f4`.
Totals after D-19450: **2,284 accepted / 24,994 not an issue / 11,246
uncertain** (38,524). Resume at D-19451. Reports:
`consolidated-context-review-default-house-of-sorrows-labels-20261012.md`,
`consolidated-context-review-default-template-warning-20261012.md`,
`consolidated-context-review-default-unused-stat-labels-20261012.md`, and
`consolidated-context-review-default-trade-marketing-title-20261012.md`.
